    PROCEDURE IsSalesFillExactCostRevLink@38(ToSalesHeader@1000 : Record 36;FromDocType@1001 : 'Sales Shipment,Sales Invoice,Sales Return Receipt,Sales Credit Memo';CurrencyCode@1002 : Code[10]) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      WITH ToSalesHeader DO
        CASE FromDocType OF
          FromDocType::"Sales Shipment":
            EXIT("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]);
          FromDocType::"Sales Invoice":
            EXIT(
              ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) AND
              ("Currency Code" = CurrencyCode));
          FromDocType::"Sales Return Receipt":
            EXIT("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Order,"Document Type"::Invoice]);
          FromDocType::"Sales Credit Memo":
            EXIT(
              ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Order,"Document Type"::Invoice]) AND
              ("Currency Code" = CurrencyCode));
        END;
      EXIT(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsPurchFillExactCostRevLink@44(ToPurchHeader@1000 : Record 38;FromDocType@1001 : 'Purchase Receipt,Purchase Invoice,Purchase Return Shipment,Purchase Credit Memo';CurrencyCode@1002 : Code[10]) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      WITH ToPurchHeader DO
        CASE FromDocType OF
          FromDocType::"Purchase Receipt":
            EXIT("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]);
          FromDocType::"Purchase Invoice":
            EXIT(
              ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) AND
              ("Currency Code" = CurrencyCode));
          FromDocType::"Purchase Return Shipment":
            EXIT("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Order,"Document Type"::Invoice]);
          FromDocType::"Purchase Credit Memo":
            EXIT(
              ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Order,"Document Type"::Invoice]) AND
              ("Currency Code" = CurrencyCode));
        END;
      EXIT(FALSE);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetSalesDocType@68(ItemLedgEntry@1000 : Record 32) : Integer;
    VAR
      SalesLine@1001 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      WITH ItemLedgEntry DO
        CASE "Document Type" OF
          "Document Type"::"Sales Shipment":
            EXIT(SalesLine."Document Type"::Order);
          "Document Type"::"Sales Invoice":
            EXIT(SalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice);
          "Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo":
            EXIT(SalesLine."Document Type"::"Credit Memo");
          "Document Type"::"Sales Return Receipt":
            EXIT(SalesLine."Document Type"::"Return Order");
        END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetPurchDocType@45(ItemLedgEntry@1000 : Record 32) : Integer;
    VAR
      PurchLine@1001 : Record 39;
    BEGIN
      WITH ItemLedgEntry DO
        CASE "Document Type" OF
          "Document Type"::"Purchase Receipt":
            EXIT(PurchLine."Document Type"::Order);
          "Document Type"::"Purchase Invoice":
            EXIT(PurchLine."Document Type"::Invoice);
          "Document Type"::"Purchase Credit Memo":
            EXIT(PurchLine."Document Type"::"Credit Memo");
          "Document Type"::"Purchase Return Shipment":
            EXIT(PurchLine."Document Type"::"Return Order");
        END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetItem@42(ItemNo@1000 : Code[20]);
    BEGIN
      IF ItemNo <> Item."No." THEN
        IF NOT Item.GET(ItemNo) THEN
          Item.INIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalcVAT@48(VAR Value@1003 : Decimal;VATPercentage@1005 : Decimal;FromPricesInclVAT@1000 : Boolean;ToPricesInclVAT@1002 : Boolean;RndgPrecision@1001 : Decimal);
    BEGIN
      IF (ToPricesInclVAT = FromPricesInclVAT) OR (Value = 0) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF ToPricesInclVAT THEN
        Value := ROUND(Value * (100 + VATPercentage) / 100,RndgPrecision)
      ELSE
        Value := ROUND(Value * 100 / (100 + VATPercentage),RndgPrecision);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ReCalcSalesLine@46(FromSalesHeader@1001 : Record 36;ToSalesHeader@1002 : Record 36;VAR SalesLine@1003 : Record 37);
    VAR
      CurrExchRate@1004 : Record 330;
      SalesLineAmount@1000 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      WITH ToSalesHeader DO BEGIN
        IF NOT IsRecalculateAmount(
             FromSalesHeader."Currency Code","Currency Code",
             FromSalesHeader."Prices Including VAT","Prices Including VAT")
        THEN
          EXIT;

        IF FromSalesHeader."Currency Code" <> "Currency Code" THEN BEGIN
          IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN
            SalesLineAmount := SalesLine."Unit Price" * SalesLine.Quantity
          ELSE
            SalesLineAmount := SalesLine."Unit Price";
          IF FromSalesHeader."Currency Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SalesLineAmount :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtFCYToLCY(
                FromSalesHeader."Posting Date",FromSalesHeader."Currency Code",
                SalesLineAmount,FromSalesHeader."Currency Factor");
            SalesLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtFCYToLCY(
                FromSalesHeader."Posting Date",FromSalesHeader."Currency Code",
                SalesLine."Line Discount Amount",FromSalesHeader."Currency Factor");
            SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtFCYToLCY(
                FromSalesHeader."Posting Date",FromSalesHeader."Currency Code",
                SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount",FromSalesHeader."Currency Factor");
          END;

          IF "Currency Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SalesLineAmount :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtLCYToFCY(
                "Posting Date","Currency Code",SalesLineAmount,"Currency Factor");
            SalesLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtLCYToFCY(
                "Posting Date","Currency Code",SalesLine."Line Discount Amount","Currency Factor");
            SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtLCYToFCY(
                "Posting Date","Currency Code",SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount","Currency Factor");
          END;
        END;

        SalesLine."Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          SalesLineAmount := ROUND(SalesLineAmount,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
          SalesLine."Unit Price" := ROUND(SalesLineAmount / SalesLine.Quantity,Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
        END ELSE
          SalesLine."Unit Price" := ROUND(SalesLineAmount,Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
        SalesLine."Line Discount Amount" := ROUND(SalesLine."Line Discount Amount",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
        SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount" := ROUND(SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");

        CalcVAT(
          SalesLine."Unit Price",SalesLine."VAT %",FromSalesHeader."Prices Including VAT",
          "Prices Including VAT",Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
        CalcVAT(
          SalesLine."Line Discount Amount",SalesLine."VAT %",FromSalesHeader."Prices Including VAT",
          "Prices Including VAT",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
        CalcVAT(
          SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount",SalesLine."VAT %",FromSalesHeader."Prices Including VAT",
          "Prices Including VAT",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ReCalcPurchLine@61(FromPurchHeader@1001 : Record 38;ToPurchHeader@1002 : Record 38;VAR PurchLine@1003 : Record 39);
    VAR
      CurrExchRate@1004 : Record 330;
      PurchLineAmount@1000 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      WITH ToPurchHeader DO BEGIN
        IF NOT IsRecalculateAmount(
             FromPurchHeader."Currency Code","Currency Code",
             FromPurchHeader."Prices Including VAT","Prices Including VAT")
        THEN
          EXIT;

        IF FromPurchHeader."Currency Code" <> "Currency Code" THEN BEGIN
          IF PurchLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN
            PurchLineAmount := PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost" * PurchLine.Quantity
          ELSE
            PurchLineAmount := PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost";
          IF FromPurchHeader."Currency Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            PurchLineAmount :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtFCYToLCY(
                FromPurchHeader."Posting Date",FromPurchHeader."Currency Code",
                PurchLineAmount,FromPurchHeader."Currency Factor");
            PurchLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtFCYToLCY(
                FromPurchHeader."Posting Date",FromPurchHeader."Currency Code",
                PurchLine."Line Discount Amount",FromPurchHeader."Currency Factor");
            PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtFCYToLCY(
                FromPurchHeader."Posting Date",FromPurchHeader."Currency Code",
                PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount",FromPurchHeader."Currency Factor");
          END;

          IF "Currency Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            PurchLineAmount :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtLCYToFCY(
                "Posting Date","Currency Code",PurchLineAmount,"Currency Factor");
            PurchLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtLCYToFCY(
                "Posting Date","Currency Code",PurchLine."Line Discount Amount","Currency Factor");
            PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount" :=
              CurrExchRate.ExchangeAmtLCYToFCY(
                "Posting Date","Currency Code",PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount","Currency Factor");
          END;
        END;

        PurchLine."Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        IF PurchLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          PurchLineAmount := ROUND(PurchLineAmount,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
          PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost" := ROUND(PurchLineAmount / PurchLine.Quantity,Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
        END ELSE
          PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost" := ROUND(PurchLineAmount,Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
        PurchLine."Line Discount Amount" := ROUND(PurchLine."Line Discount Amount",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
        PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount" := ROUND(PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");

        CalcVAT(
          PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost",PurchLine."VAT %",FromPurchHeader."Prices Including VAT",
          "Prices Including VAT",Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
        CalcVAT(
          PurchLine."Line Discount Amount",PurchLine."VAT %",FromPurchHeader."Prices Including VAT",
          "Prices Including VAT",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
        CalcVAT(
          PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount",PurchLine."VAT %",FromPurchHeader."Prices Including VAT",
          "Prices Including VAT",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE IsRecalculateAmount@63(FromCurrencyCode@1000 : Code[10];ToCurrencyCode@1002 : Code[10];FromPricesInclVAT@1001 : Boolean;ToPricesInclVAT@1003 : Boolean) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(
        (FromCurrencyCode <> ToCurrencyCode) OR
        (FromPricesInclVAT <> ToPricesInclVAT));
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateRevSalesLineAmount@67(VAR SalesLine@1000 : Record 37;OrgQtyBase@1009 : Decimal;FromPricesInclVAT@1005 : Boolean;ToPricesInclVAT@1006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Amount@1007 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      IF (OrgQtyBase = 0) OR (SalesLine.Quantity = 0) THEN
        EXIT;

      Amount := SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Unit Price";
      CalcVAT(
        Amount,SalesLine."VAT %",FromPricesInclVAT,ToPricesInclVAT,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      SalesLine."Unit Price" := Amount / SalesLine.Quantity;
      // --> 20131112-WI00-CMC02.02.00-0819-DRN01
      //BEFORE
      //SalesLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
      //  SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Unit Price" * (SalesLine."Line Discount %" / 100);
      //AFTER
      SalesLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
        ROUND(
          ROUND(SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Unit Price",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision") *
          SalesLine."Line Discount %" / 100,
          Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      // <-- 20131112-WI00-CMC02.02.00-0819-DRN01
      Amount := ROUND(SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount" / OrgQtyBase * SalesLine."Quantity (Base)",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      CalcVAT(
        Amount,SalesLine."VAT %",FromPricesInclVAT,ToPricesInclVAT,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount" := Amount;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalculateRevSalesLineAmount@84(VAR SalesLine@1000 : Record 37;OrgQtyBase@1009 : Decimal;FromPricesInclVAT@1005 : Boolean;ToPricesInclVAT@1006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      UnitPrice@1007 : Decimal;
      LineDiscAmt@1001 : Decimal;
      InvDiscAmt@1002 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      UpdateRevSalesLineAmount(SalesLine,OrgQtyBase,FromPricesInclVAT,ToPricesInclVAT);

      UnitPrice := SalesLine."Unit Price";
      LineDiscAmt := SalesLine."Line Discount Amount";
      InvDiscAmt := SalesLine."Inv. Discount Amount";

      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line Discount Amount",LineDiscAmt);
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Inv. Discount Amount",InvDiscAmt);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateRevPurchLineAmount@65(VAR PurchLine@1000 : Record 39;OrgQtyBase@1009 : Decimal;FromPricesInclVAT@1005 : Boolean;ToPricesInclVAT@1006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Amount@1007 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      IF (OrgQtyBase = 0) OR (PurchLine.Quantity = 0) THEN
        EXIT;

      Amount := PurchLine.Quantity * PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost";
      CalcVAT(
        Amount,PurchLine."VAT %",FromPricesInclVAT,ToPricesInclVAT,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost" := Amount / PurchLine.Quantity;
      PurchLine."Line Discount Amount" :=
        PurchLine.Quantity * PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost" * (PurchLine."Line Discount %" / 100);
      Amount := ROUND(PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount" / OrgQtyBase * PurchLine."Quantity (Base)",Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      CalcVAT(
        Amount,PurchLine."VAT %",FromPricesInclVAT,ToPricesInclVAT,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
      PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount" := Amount;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalculateRevPurchLineAmount@82(VAR PurchLine@1000 : Record 39;OrgQtyBase@1009 : Decimal;FromPricesInclVAT@1005 : Boolean;ToPricesInclVAT@1006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      DirectUnitCost@1007 : Decimal;
      LineDiscAmt@1001 : Decimal;
      InvDiscAmt@1002 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      UpdateRevPurchLineAmount(PurchLine,OrgQtyBase,FromPricesInclVAT,ToPricesInclVAT);

      DirectUnitCost := PurchLine."Direct Unit Cost";
      LineDiscAmt := PurchLine."Line Discount Amount";
      InvDiscAmt := PurchLine."Inv. Discount Amount";

      PurchLine.VALIDATE("Direct Unit Cost",DirectUnitCost);
      PurchLine.VALIDATE("Line Discount Amount",LineDiscAmt);
      PurchLine.VALIDATE("Inv. Discount Amount",InvDiscAmt);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InitCurrency@81(CurrencyCode@1000 : Code[10]);
    BEGIN
      IF CurrencyCode <> '' THEN
        Currency.GET(CurrencyCode)
      ELSE
        Currency.InitRoundingPrecision;

      Currency.TESTFIELD("Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
      Currency.TESTFIELD("Amount Rounding Precision");
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE OpenWindow@79();
    BEGIN
      Window.OPEN(
        Text022 +
        Text023 +
        Text024);
      WindowUpdateTime := TIME;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE IsTimeForUpdate@75() : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF TIME - WindowUpdateTime >= 1000 THEN BEGIN
        WindowUpdateTime := TIME;
        EXIT(TRUE);
      END;
      EXIT(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UTlocalCall@60(localFunctionName@1000 : Text[30];VAR param@1001 : ARRAY [20] OF Variant;VAR return@1002 : Variant);
    VAR
      ItemLedgEntry@1014 : Record 32;
      ItemLedgEntryBuf@1007 : TEMPORARY Record 32;
      ToSalesHeader@1003 : Record 36;
      FromSalesHeader@1004 : Record 36;
      FromSalesLineBuf@1005 : TEMPORARY Record 37;
      FromSalesLine@1006 : Record 37;
      ToPurchHeader@1018 : Record 38;
      FromPurchHeader@1019 : Record 38;
      FromPurchLineBuf@1021 : TEMPORARY Record 39;
      FromPurchLine@1020 : Record 39;
      FromSalesShptLine@1008 : Record 111;
      FromSalesInvLine@1013 : Record 113;
      FromReturnRcptLine@1017 : Record 6661;
      FromPurchRcptLine@1022 : Record 121;
      FromPurchInvLine@1023 : Record 123;
      FromReturnShptLine@1024 : Record 6651;
      TempItemTrkgEntry@1026 : TEMPORARY Record 337;
      FromDocType@1010 : Integer;
      i@1009 : Integer;
      NextLineNo@1025 : Integer;
      NextItemTrkgEntryNo@1027 : Integer;
      CopyItemTrkg@1011 : Boolean;
      MissingExCostRevLink@1012 : Boolean;
      IsSplitILE@1015 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CASE localFunctionName OF
        'SplitPstdSalesLinesPerILE':
          BEGIN
            ToSalesHeader := param[1];
            FromSalesHeader := param[2];
            FromDocType := param[3];
            FromDocType := FromDocType - 1;
            CASE FromDocType OF
              0:
                BEGIN
                  FromSalesShptLine := param[4];
                  FromSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromSalesShptLine);
                  FromSalesShptLine.FilterPstdDocLnItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntry);

                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPstdSalesLinesPerILE(
                      ToSalesHeader,FromSalesHeader,ItemLedgEntry,
                      FromSalesLineBuf,FromSalesLine,NextLineNo,CopyItemTrkg,MissingExCostRevLink,
                      IsSalesFillExactCostRevLink(ToSalesHeader,FromDocType,FromSalesHeader."Currency Code"),TRUE);
                END;
              1:
                BEGIN
                  FromSalesInvLine := param[4];
                  FromSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromSalesInvLine);
                  FromSalesInvLine.GetItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntryBuf,TRUE);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPstdSalesLinesPerILE(
                      ToSalesHeader,FromSalesHeader,ItemLedgEntryBuf,
                      FromSalesLineBuf,FromSalesLine,NextLineNo,CopyItemTrkg,MissingExCostRevLink,
                      IsSalesFillExactCostRevLink(ToSalesHeader,FromDocType,FromSalesHeader."Currency Code"),FALSE);
                END;
            END;
            param[5] := CopyItemTrkg;
            param[6] := MissingExCostRevLink;
            i := 10;
            IF IsSplitILE THEN BEGIN
              return := TRUE;
              FromSalesLineBuf.FINDSET;
              REPEAT
                param[i] := FromSalesLineBuf;
                i := i + 1;
              UNTIL FromSalesLineBuf.NEXT = 0;
              param[8] := i - 10;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              return := FALSE;
              param[8] := 1;
              param[i] := FromSalesLineBuf;
            END;
          END;

        'SplitSalesDocLinesPerItemTrkg':
          BEGIN
            FromDocType := param[1];
            CASE FromDocType OF
              1:
                BEGIN
                  FromSalesShptLine := param[2];
                  FromSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromSalesShptLine);
                  FromSalesShptLine.FilterPstdDocLnItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntry);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitSalesDocLinesPerItemTrkg(
                      ItemLedgEntry,TempItemTrkgEntry,FromSalesLineBuf,FromSalesLine,
                      NextLineNo,NextItemTrkgEntryNo,MissingExCostRevLink,TRUE);
                END;
              2:
                BEGIN
                  FromSalesInvLine := param[2];
                  FromSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromSalesInvLine);
                  FromSalesInvLine.GetItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntryBuf,TRUE);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitSalesDocLinesPerItemTrkg(
                      ItemLedgEntryBuf,TempItemTrkgEntry,FromSalesLineBuf,
                      FromSalesLine,NextLineNo,NextItemTrkgEntryNo,MissingExCostRevLink,FALSE);
                END;
              3:
                BEGIN
                  FromReturnRcptLine := param[2];
                  FromSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromReturnRcptLine);
                  FromReturnRcptLine.FilterPstdDocLnItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntry);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitSalesDocLinesPerItemTrkg(
                      ItemLedgEntry,TempItemTrkgEntry,FromSalesLineBuf,
                      FromSalesLine,NextLineNo,NextItemTrkgEntryNo,MissingExCostRevLink,TRUE);
                END;
            END;
            param[3] := MissingExCostRevLink;
            i := 5;
            TempItemTrkgEntry.RESET;
            TempItemTrkgEntry.FINDSET;
            REPEAT
              param[i] := TempItemTrkgEntry;
              i := i + 1;
            UNTIL TempItemTrkgEntry.NEXT = 0;
            param[4] := i - 5;

            IF IsSplitILE THEN BEGIN
              return := TRUE;
              i := 12;
              FromSalesLineBuf.FINDSET;
              REPEAT
                param[i] := FromSalesLineBuf;
                i := i + 1;
              UNTIL FromSalesLineBuf.NEXT = 0;
              param[11] := i - 12;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              return := FALSE;
              param[11] := 1;
              param[12] := FromSalesLineBuf;
            END;
          END;
        'SplitPstdPurchLinesPerILE':
          BEGIN
            ToPurchHeader := param[1];
            FromPurchHeader := param[2];
            FromDocType := param[3];
            FromDocType := FromDocType - 1;
            CASE FromDocType OF
              0:
                BEGIN
                  FromPurchRcptLine := param[4];
                  FromPurchLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromPurchRcptLine);
                  FromPurchRcptLine.FilterPstdDocLnItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntry);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPstdPurchLinesPerILE(
                      ToPurchHeader,FromPurchHeader,ItemLedgEntry,
                      FromPurchLineBuf,FromPurchLine,NextLineNo,CopyItemTrkg,MissingExCostRevLink,
                      IsPurchFillExactCostRevLink(ToPurchHeader,FromDocType,FromPurchHeader."Currency Code"),TRUE);
                END;
              1:
                BEGIN
                  FromPurchInvLine := param[4];
                  FromPurchLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromPurchInvLine);
                  FromPurchInvLine.GetItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntryBuf,TRUE);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPstdPurchLinesPerILE(
                      ToPurchHeader,FromPurchHeader,ItemLedgEntryBuf,
                      FromPurchLineBuf,FromPurchLine,NextLineNo,CopyItemTrkg,MissingExCostRevLink,
                      IsPurchFillExactCostRevLink(ToPurchHeader,FromDocType,FromPurchHeader."Currency Code"),FALSE);
                END;
            END;
            param[5] := CopyItemTrkg;
            param[6] := MissingExCostRevLink;
            i := 10;
            IF IsSplitILE THEN BEGIN
              return := TRUE;
              FromPurchLineBuf.FINDSET;
              REPEAT
                param[i] := FromPurchLineBuf;
                i := i + 1;
              UNTIL FromPurchLineBuf.NEXT = 0;
              param[8] := i - 10;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              return := FALSE;
              param[8] := 1;
              param[i] := FromPurchLineBuf;
            END;
          END;
        'SplitPurchDocLinesPerItemTrkg':
          BEGIN
            FromDocType := param[1];
            CASE FromDocType OF
              1:
                BEGIN
                  FromPurchRcptLine := param[2];
                  FromPurchLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromPurchRcptLine);
                  FromPurchRcptLine.FilterPstdDocLnItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntry);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPurchDocLinesPerItemTrkg(
                      ItemLedgEntry,TempItemTrkgEntry,FromPurchLineBuf,
                      FromPurchLine,NextLineNo,NextItemTrkgEntryNo,MissingExCostRevLink,TRUE);
                END;
              2:
                BEGIN
                  FromPurchInvLine := param[2];
                  FromPurchLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromPurchInvLine);
                  FromPurchInvLine.GetItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntryBuf,TRUE);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPurchDocLinesPerItemTrkg(
                      ItemLedgEntryBuf,TempItemTrkgEntry,FromPurchLineBuf,
                      FromPurchLine,NextLineNo,NextItemTrkgEntryNo,MissingExCostRevLink,FALSE);
                END;
              3:
                BEGIN
                  FromReturnShptLine := param[2];
                  FromPurchLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromReturnShptLine);
                  FromReturnShptLine.FilterPstdDocLnItemLedgEntries(ItemLedgEntry);
                  IsSplitILE :=
                    SplitPurchDocLinesPerItemTrkg(
                      ItemLedgEntry,TempItemTrkgEntry,FromPurchLineBuf,
                      FromPurchLine,NextLineNo,NextItemTrkgEntryNo,MissingExCostRevLink,TRUE);
                END;
            END;
            param[3] := MissingExCostRevLink;
            i := 5;
            TempItemTrkgEntry.RESET;
            TempItemTrkgEntry.FINDSET;
            REPEAT
              param[i] := TempItemTrkgEntry;
              i := i + 1;
            UNTIL TempItemTrkgEntry.NEXT = 0;
            param[4] := i - 5;

            IF IsSplitILE THEN BEGIN
              return := TRUE;
              i := 13;
              FromPurchLineBuf.FINDSET;
              REPEAT
                param[i] := FromPurchLineBuf;
                i := i + 1;
              UNTIL FromPurchLineBuf.NEXT = 0;
              param[12] := i - 13;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              return := FALSE;
              param[12] := 1;
              param[13] := FromPurchLineBuf;
            END;
          END;
        ELSE
          ERROR('Local function %1 is not included for test.',localFunctionName);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ConfirmApply@32();
    BEGIN
      AskApply := FALSE;
      ApplyFully := FALSE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ConvertFromBase@47(VAR Quantity@1000 : Decimal;"Quantity (Base)"@1001 : Decimal;"Qty. per Unit of Measure"@1002 : Decimal);
    BEGIN
      IF "Qty. per Unit of Measure" = 0 THEN
        Quantity := "Quantity (Base)"
      ELSE
        Quantity :=
          ROUND("Quantity (Base)" / "Qty. per Unit of Measure",0.00001);
    END;

    PROCEDURE Sign@77(Quantity@1000 : Decimal) : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      IF Quantity<0 THEN
        EXIT(-1);
      EXIT(1);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ShowMessageReapply@80(OriginalQuantity@1000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Text@1001 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      Text := '';
      IF SkippedLine THEN
        Text := Text029;
      IF OriginalQuantity AND ReappDone THEN
        IF Text = '' THEN
          Text := Text025;
      IF SomeAreFixed THEN
        MESSAGE(Text031);
      IF Text <> '' THEN
        MESSAGE(Text);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CopyFromKitSalesToLine@1462000(VAR ToSalesLine@1462000 : Record 37;VAR FromSalesLine@1462001 : Record 37);
    VAR
      FromKitSalesLine@1462002 : Record 25000;
      ToKitSalesLine@1462003 : Record 25000;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT RecalculateLines THEN
        IF ToSalesLine."Build Kit" THEN BEGIN
          FromKitSalesLine.RESET;
          FromKitSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",FromSalesLine."Document Type");
          FromKitSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",FromSalesLine."Document No.");
          FromKitSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",FromSalesLine."Line No.");
          IF FromKitSalesLine.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              ToKitSalesLine := FromKitSalesLine;
              ToKitSalesLine."Document Type" := ToSalesLine."Document Type";
              ToKitSalesLine."Document No." := ToSalesLine."Document No.";
              ToKitSalesLine."Document Line No." := ToSalesLine."Line No.";
              ToKitSalesLine.INSERT;
            UNTIL FromKitSalesLine.NEXT = 0;
        END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CopyFromKitSalesShptToLine@1462001(VAR ToSalesLine@1462000 : Record 37;VAR FromSalesShptLine@1462001 : Record 111);
    VAR
      FromKitSalesShptLine@1462002 : Record 25004;
      ToKitSalesLine@1462003 : Record 25000;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT RecalculateLines THEN
        IF ToSalesLine."Build Kit" THEN BEGIN
          FromKitSalesShptLine.RESET;
          FromKitSalesShptLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",FromSalesShptLine."Document No.");
          FromKitSalesShptLine.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",FromSalesShptLine."Line No.");
          IF FromKitSalesShptLine.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              ToKitSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(FromKitSalesShptLine);
              ToKitSalesLine."Document Type" := ToSalesLine."Document Type";
              ToKitSalesLine."Document No." := ToSalesLine."Document No.";
              ToKitSalesLine."Document Line No." := ToSalesLine."Line No.";
              CASE ToKitSalesLine.Type OF
                ToKitSalesLine.Type::Item,ToKitSalesLine.Type::Resource:
                  BEGIN
                    ToKitSalesLine."Extended Quantity" := ToKitSalesLine."Quantity per" * ToSalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                    ToKitSalesLine."Extended Quantity (Base)" := ToKitSalesLine."Quantity per (Base)" * ToSalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                  END;
                ToKitSalesLine.Type::"Setup Resource":
                  BEGIN
                    ToKitSalesLine."Extended Quantity" := ToKitSalesLine."Quantity per";
                    ToKitSalesLine."Extended Quantity (Base)" := ToKitSalesLine."Quantity per (Base)";
                  END;
              END;
              ToKitSalesLine."Outstanding Quantity" := ToKitSalesLine."Extended Quantity";
              ToKitSalesLine."Outstanding Qty. (Base)" := ToKitSalesLine."Extended Quantity (Base)";
              ToKitSalesLine.INSERT;
            UNTIL FromKitSalesShptLine.NEXT = 0;
        END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ItemReturnCheck@8053001(ItemNo_P@8053002 : Code[20];VariantCode_P@8053004 : Code[10]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      UserSetup_L@8053000 : Record 91;
      Found_L@8053003 : Boolean;
      Item_L@8053001 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20130821-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
      UserSetup_L.GET(USERID);
      UserSetup_L.TESTFIELD("User Groupe Code");
      // --> 20140429-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2009-BRA01
      //BEFORE
      {
      // ** recherche param'trage article ** //
      ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
      ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
      ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Item);
      ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",ItemNo_P);
      Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
      }
      //AFTER
      //Recherche . la variante
      IF VariantCode_P<>'' THEN
        BEGIN
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Item);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",ItemNo_P);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("Variant Code",VariantCode_P);
          Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
        END;
      IF NOT Found_L THEN
        BEGIN
          // ** recherche param'trage article ** //
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Item);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",ItemNo_P);
          // --> 20140521-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2009-FEC01
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("Variant Code",'');
          // --< 20140521-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2009-FEC01
          Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
        END;
      // <-- 20140429-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2009-BRA01

      // --> 20130905-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-BRA01
      //BEFORE
      //Item_L.GET(ItemNo_P);
      //After
      IF NOT Item_L.GET(ItemNo_P) THEN
       EXIT(FALSE);
      // <-- 20130905-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-BRA01
      IF NOT Found_L THEN
        BEGIN
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Species);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",Item_L."Species Code");
          Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
        END;
      IF NOT Found_L THEN
        BEGIN
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Subfamily);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",Item_L."Sub-Family Code");
          Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
        END;
      IF NOT Found_L THEN
        BEGIN
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Family);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",Item_L."Family Code");
          Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
        END;
      IF NOT Found_L THEN
        BEGIN
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.RESET;
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("User Group Code",UserSetup_L."User Groupe Code");
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE(Type,ItemReturnSetupRec_G.Type::Activity);
          ItemReturnSetupRec_G.SETRANGE("No.",Item_L."Activity Code");
          Found_L := ItemReturnSetupRec_G.FINDFIRST;
        END;
      EXIT(Found_L);
      // <-- 20130821-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckItemReturnSetupForShip@8053003(VAR FromSalesShptLine_P@8053001 : Record 111;VAR ToSalesHeader_P@8053003 : Record 36);
    VAR
      CMCSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8052982;
      SalesShptHeader_L@8053002 : Record 110;
      recCust_L@60000 : Record 18;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140424-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2023-FEC05

      CMCSetup_L.GET;
      IF NOT CMCSetup_L."Activate Item Return Check" THEN
        EXIT;

      // --> 20140725-APP06-TER01.01.00-FHS01
      recCust_L.GET(FromSalesShptLine_P."Bill-to Customer No.");
      IF recCust_L."Do Not Allow Discount" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
      // <-- 20140725-APP06-TER01.01.00-FHS01
        WITH FromSalesShptLine_P DO BEGIN
          IF FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              IF Type = Type::Item THEN BEGIN
                // --> 20140429-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2009-BRA01
                //BEFORE
                //IF ItemReturnCheck("No.") THEN BEGIN
                //AFTER
                IF ItemReturnCheck("No.","Variant Code") THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20140429-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2009-BRA01
                  ItemReturnSetupRec_G.TESTFIELD("Authorize Shipment Extract");
                  SalesShptHeader_L.GET("Document No.");
                  IF FORMAT(ItemReturnSetupRec_G."Max. Shipment Date Calculation")<> ''THEN
                  IF CALCDATE(ItemReturnSetupRec_G."Max. Shipment Date Calculation",ToSalesHeader_P."Order Date")
                                > SalesShptHeader_L."Posting Date"  THEN
                      ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(MsgErr8053000_G,TABLECAPTION,"Line No."));
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Text8053020);
              END;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      // --> 20140725-APP06-TER01.01.00-FHS01
      END;
      // <-- 20140725-APP06-TER01.01.00-FHS01

      // --< 20140424-Gen13-CMC02.02.00-2023-FEC05
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckItemReturnSetupForPostInv@8053004(VAR FromSalesInvLine_P@8053001 : Record 113;VAR ToSalesHeader_P@8053003 : Record 36);
    VAR
      CMCSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8052982;
      SalesInvHeader_L@8053002 : Record 112;
      SalesShipHeader_L@8053004 : Record 110;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20141010-GEN13-CMC02.02.00-FDA01
      CMCSetup_L.GET;
      IF NOT CMCSetup_L."Activate Item Return Check" THEN
        EXIT;

      WITH FromSalesInvLine_P DO BEGIN
        IF FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            IF Type = Type::Item THEN BEGIN
              IF ItemReturnCheck("No.","Variant Code") THEN BEGIN
                ItemReturnSetupRec_G.TESTFIELD("Authorize Invoice Extract");
                SalesInvHeader_L.GET("Document No.");
                IF SalesShipHeader_L.GET("Shipment No.") THEN BEGIN
                  IF FORMAT(ItemReturnSetupRec_G."Max. Shipment Date Calculation") <> '' THEN
                    IF CALCDATE(ItemReturnSetupRec_G."Max. Shipment Date Calculation",ToSalesHeader_P."Order Date")
                                                                                      > SalesShipHeader_L."Posting Date" THEN
                      ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(MsgErr8053000_G,TABLECAPTION,"Line No."));
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF FORMAT(ItemReturnSetupRec_G."Max. Shipment Date Calculation") <> '' THEN
                    IF CALCDATE(ItemReturnSetupRec_G."Max. Shipment Date Calculation",ToSalesHeader_P."Order Date")
                                                                                > SalesInvHeader_L."Posting Date"  THEN
                      ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(MsgErr8053000_G,TABLECAPTION,"Line No."));
                END;
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Text8053020);
            END;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // <-- 20141010-GEN13-CMC02.02.00-FDA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetStoredLink@1000000001(FromSalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37) Result : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // 20150611-wi00-CMC02.02.00-12573-3024-JLT01 : MODCODE  (provoquait un n› de ligne origine Produit Gratuit)
      // --> 20160122-APP19-ADV01.01.00-2525-DAD02
      {BEFORE
      IF FromSalesLine_P."Free Item Root Line" = 0 THEN EXIT;

      IF Templine.GET(FromSalesLine_P."Document Type",FromSalesLine_P."Document No.",FromSalesLine_P."Free Item Root Line") THEN
        Result :=Templine."Appl.-to Item Entry"; // Nouveau N› de la lignen g'n'r'e
      AFTER}
      // <-- 20160122-APP19-ADV01.01.00-2525-DAD02
      IF FromSalesLine_P."Disc. Root Line" = 0  THEN
        IF Templine.GET(FromSalesLine_P."Document Type",FromSalesLine_P."Document No.",FromSalesLine_P."Free Item Root Line") THEN
          Result := Templine."Appl.-to Item Entry"; // Nouveau N› de la ligne g'n'r'e
      IF FromSalesLine_P."Free Item Root Line" = 0  THEN
        IF Templine.GET(FromSalesLine_P."Document Type",FromSalesLine_P."Document No.",FromSalesLine_P."Disc. Root Line") THEN
          Result := Templine."Appl.-to Item Entry"; // Nouveau N› de la ligne g'n'r'e
      // <-- 20160122-APP19-ADV01.01.00-2525-DAD02
      EXIT(Result);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSalesItemChargeRootLineNo@1000000002(FromSalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37) LineNo_R : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20160113-Nut02-ADV01.01.00-2428-JCD01
      IF FromSalesLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No." <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF Templine.GET(FromSalesLine_P."Document Type",FromSalesLine_P."Document No.",FromSalesLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No.") THEN
          LineNo_R :=Templine."Appl.-to Item Entry"
        //Type doc = quote si copie autre table 37
        ELSE IF Templine.GET(Templine."Document Type"::Quote,FromSalesLine_P."Document No.",
                  FromSalesLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No.") THEN
          LineNo_R :=Templine."Appl.-to Item Entry";
      END;
      EXIT(LineNo_R);
      // <-- 20160113-Nut02-ADV01.01.00-2428-JCD01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetPurchItemChargeRootLineNo@1000000003(FromPurchLine_P@1000000000 : Record 39) LineNo_R : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20160113-Nut02-ADV01.01.00-2428-JCD01
      IF FromPurchLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No." <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF TempPurchLine_G.GET(FromPurchLine_P."Document Type",FromPurchLine_P."Document No.",
          FromPurchLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No.") THEN
          LineNo_R :=TempPurchLine_G."Appl.-to Item Entry"
        ELSE IF TempPurchLine_G.GET(TempPurchLine_G."Document Type"::Quote,FromPurchLine_P."Document No.",
                  FromPurchLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No.") THEN
          LineNo_R :=TempPurchLine_G."Appl.-to Item Entry";
      END;
      //FromPurchLine_P.FINDFIRST;
      //MESSAGE('%1 %2',TempPurchLine_G."Appl.-to Item Entry",TempPurchLine_G);
      EXIT(LineNo_R);
      // <-- 20160113-Nut02-ADV01.01.00-2428-JCD01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetNextSaleLine@1000000000(SalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37) LineNo_L : Integer;
    VAR
      SalesLine_L@1000000001 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20160707-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3133-JCD01
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesLine_P."Document Type");
      SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine_P."Document No.");
      IF SalesLine_L.FINDLAST THEN
        LineNo_L := SalesLine_L."Line No." + 10000
      ELSE
        LineNo_L := SalesLine_P."Line No." + 10000;
      // <-- 20160707-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3133-JCD01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetShipmentLocation@1000000004(No_P@1000000000 : Code[20];LineNo_P@1000000001 : Integer) : Code[10];
    VAR
      SalesShipmentLine_L@1000000002 : Record 111;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20161220-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-1548-DDS01
      IF SalesShipmentLine_L.GET(No_P,LineNo_P) THEN
        EXIT(SalesShipmentLine_L."Location Code")
      // <-- 20161220-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-1548-DDS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetKitLinesFromShipment@60000(ToSalesLine_P@60000 : Record 37;FromSalesShptLine_P@60001 : Record 111);
    VAR
      Item_L@60002 : Record 27;
      ParamPhytos_L@60003 : Record 60076;
      KitSalesShipmentLine_L@60004 : Record 25004;
      KitSalesLine_L@60005 : Record 25000;
      ItemLedgerEntry2_L@60006 : Record 32;
      T337_L@60007 : Record 337;
      RecReservEntry1_L@60008 : Record 337;
      ManageTracking_L@60009 : Boolean;
      QtyNotReturned_L@60010 : Decimal;
      RevUnitCostLCY_L@60011 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20171221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      //on ne gSre que si l'on est en train d'extraire une exp'dition
      IF (FromSalesShptLine_P."Document No." = '') OR (FromSalesShptLine_P."Line No." = 0) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF FromSalesShptLine_P.Type <> FromSalesShptLine_P.Type::Item THEN
        EXIT;
      IF NOT FromSalesShptLine_P."Build Kit" THEN
        EXIT;
      //on ne fait que si l'article est param'tr' pour g'rer les composants en retour

      Item_L.GET(FromSalesShptLine_P."No.");
      IF Item_L."No return for kit component" THEN
        EXIT;

      //SalesOrderLine_L.GET(SalesOrderLine_L."Document Type"::Order,OldSalesShptLine_P."Order No.",OldSalesShptLine_P."Order Line No.");
      //utilisation d'une feuille d'assemblage param'tr'e
      ParamPhytos_L.GET;
      //IF ParamPhytos_L."Modele nomencl annulation BL" = '' THEN
        //ERROR(Text60001);


      //si pas de kit exp'di' alors pas de d'sassemblage
      KitSalesShipmentLine_L.RESET;
      KitSalesShipmentLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",FromSalesShptLine_P."Document No.");
      KitSalesShipmentLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",FromSalesShptLine_P."Line No.");
      IF KitSalesShipmentLine_L.ISEMPTY
        THEN EXIT;


      //MESSAGE('Retour article %1, kit . prendre en compte',FromSalesShptLine_P."No.");
      //si l'on arrive ici alors c'est qu'on doit g'rer le kitting trouv' sur l'exp'dition originale
      //au cas o-, on supprime les 'ventuelles lignes kit existantes

      // --> 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      FromSalesShptLine_P.CalcShippedSaleNotReturned(QtyNotReturned_L,RevUnitCostLCY_L,FALSE);
      IF FromSalesShptLine_P.Quantity - QtyNotReturned_L = 0 THEN
        ManageTracking_L := TRUE;
      // <-- 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01


      IF KitSalesShipmentLine_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        //Cr'ation lignes de kit retour

        KitSalesLine_L.INIT;
        KitSalesLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(KitSalesShipmentLine_L);
        KitSalesLine_L."Document Type" := KitSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Return Order";
        KitSalesLine_L."Document No." := ToSalesLine_P."Document No.";
        KitSalesLine_L."Location Code" := ToSalesLine_P."Location Code";
        KitSalesLine_L."Document Line No." := ToSalesLine_P."Line No.";
        KitSalesLine_L."Outstanding Quantity" := KitSalesLine_L."Quantity per" * ToSalesLine_P."Outstanding Qty. (Base)";
        KitSalesLine_L."Outstanding Qty. (Base)" := KitSalesLine_L."Quantity per (Base)" * ToSalesLine_P."Outstanding Qty. (Base)";
        // --> 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        KitSalesLine_L."Extended Quantity" := KitSalesLine_L."Quantity per" * ToSalesLine_P."Quantity (Base)";
        KitSalesLine_L."Extended Quantity (Base)" := KitSalesLine_L."Quantity per (Base)" * ToSalesLine_P."Quantity (Base)";
        // <-- 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        KitSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        //Cr'ation de la traÎabilit' sur la base de celle existante en exp' sur cette meme ligne de kit
        //on ne cherche a g'rer la traÎa composnt que si on retourne la totalit' de l'exp' originale
        // --> 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        IF ManageTracking_L THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.RESET;
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",FromSalesShptLine_P."Document No.");
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",FromSalesShptLine_P."Line No.");
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Entry Type",ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Entry Type"::Consumption);
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Item No.",KitSalesLine_L."No.");
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code",KitSalesLine_L."Variant Code");
        ItemLedgerEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Lot No.",'<>%1','');
        IF ItemLedgerEntry2_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          KitSalesLine_L.RESET;
          KitSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",KitSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Return Order");
          KitSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(KitSalesLine_L."Document No.",ToSalesLine_P."Document No.");
          KitSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(KitSalesLine_L."Document Line No.",ToSalesLine_P."Line No.");
          KitSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,KitSalesLine_L.Type::Item);
          KitSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("No.",KitSalesLine_L."No.");
          KitSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code",KitSalesLine_L."Variant Code");
          IF KitSalesLine_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            //A t'on d'j. une ligne traca en T337 correspondante ou doit on la cr'er ?
            T337_L.RESET;
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Source ID",ToSalesLine_P."Document No.");
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",25000);
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Source Subtype",5);
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Source Prod. Order Line",ToSalesLine_P."Line No.");
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Source Ref. No.",KitSalesLine_L."Line No.");


            T337_L.SETRANGE("Item No.",KitSalesLine_L."No.");
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code",KitSalesLine_L."Variant Code");
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Location Code", ToSalesLine_P."Location Code");
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Reservation Status",T337_L."Reservation Status"::Prospect);
            T337_L.SETRANGE("Lot No.",ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Lot No.");
            IF NOT T337_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
              //cr'ation
              T337_L.INIT;

              RecReservEntry1_L.RESET;
              IF RecReservEntry1_L.FINDLAST THEN
                T337_L."Entry No." := RecReservEntry1_L."Entry No." + 1
              ELSE
                T337_L."Entry No." := 1;
              T337_L."Source ID":= ToSalesLine_P."Document No.";
              T337_L."Source Ref. No." := KitSalesLine_L."Line No.";
              T337_L."Source Type" := 25000;
              T337_L."Source Subtype" := 5;
              T337_L."Source Prod. Order Line" := ToSalesLine_P."Line No.";
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Item No.",KitSalesLine_L."No.");
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code",KitSalesLine_L."Variant Code");
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Location Code",ToSalesLine_P."Location Code");
              T337_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure";
              T337_L."Item Tracking" := T337_L."Item Tracking" :: "Lot No.";
              T337_L."Lot No." := ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Lot No.";
              T337_L."Reservation Status" := T337_L."Reservation Status" :: Prospect;
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Quantity (Base)",ABS(ItemLedgerEntry2_L.Quantity));
              T337_L."Quantity Invoiced (Base)" := 0;
              T337_L.Positive := (T337_L."Quantity (Base)" > 0);
              T337_L."Creation Date" := WORKDATE;
              T337_L."Created By" := USERID;
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Manufacturing Date",ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Manufacturing Date");
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Expiration Date",ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Expiration Date");
              //T337_L."Shipment Date" := ToSalesLine_P."Shipment Date";
              T337_L.Description := ItemLedgerEntry2_L.Description;
              T337_L."Appl.-to Item Entry" := ItemLedgerEntry2_L."Entry No.";
              T337_L.INSERT;


            END
            ELSE BEGIN
              //maj
              //si on trouve un ligne T337 d'ja pr'sent, c'est qu'on est ds le cas d'un meme lot d'coup'
              //en plusieur ecriture article : mais la ltraÎa doit ^tre port'e par une ligne unique
              //T337_L.VALIDATE("Quantity (Base)",ItemLedgerEntry2_L.Quantity);
              T337_L.VALIDATE("Quantity (Base)",T337_L."Quantity (Base)" + ABS(ItemLedgerEntry2_L.Quantity));
              T337_L.MODIFY;

            END;

          UNTIL KitSalesLine_L.NEXT = 0;
        UNTIL ItemLedgerEntry2_L.NEXT = 0;
        // --> 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        END;
        // <-- 20180103-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      UNTIL KitSalesShipmentLine_L.NEXT = 0;

      //< -- 20171221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      LS = changes made by LS Retail
      // --> 3LI BEGIN
